import hashlib

bp = Blueprint('account', __name__)
EMAIL_RE = re.compile(r'^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$')
ALPHABET = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789'          # ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† O/0/I/1 طھط§ ط§ط´طھط¨ط§ظ‡ ط®ظˆط§ظ†ط¯ظ‡ ظ†ط´ظˆط¯
REQUIRED_TABLES = {'users', 'workshops', 'farming_years', 'activities', 'materials', 'daily_works', 'work_materials'}
MAX_UNPACKED = 2 * 1024 ** 3
_fails = defaultdict(list)       # (ظ†ظˆط¹طŒ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±) -> ط²ظ…ط§ظ† طھظ„ط§ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ†ط§ظ…ظˆظپظ‚


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ط§ط¨ط²ط§ط±ظ‡ط§
def _ok(**kw):
    return jsonify(dict(success=True, **kw))


def _bad(msg, code=400):
    return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': msg}), code


def _body():
    d = request.get_json(silent=True)
    return d if isinstance(d, dict) else {}


def _throttled(kind):
    now = time.time()
    key = (kind, current_user.id)
    _fails[key] = [t for t in _fails[key] if now - t < 600]
    return len(_fails[key]) >= 5


def _record_fail(kind):
    _fails[(kind, current_user.id)].append(time.time())


def _need_password(d):
    if _throttled('pw'):
        return _bad('طھظ„ط§ط´ ظ†ط§ظ…ظˆظپظ‚ ط²غŒط§ط¯ ط¨ظˆط¯ط› ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط¯ظˆط¨ط§ط±ظ‡ ط§ظ…طھط­ط§ظ† ع©ظ†غŒط¯.', 429)
    if not current_user.check_password(d.get('current_password') or ''):
        _record_fail('pw')
        return _bad('ط±ظ…ط² ط¹ط¨ظˆط± ظپط¹ظ„غŒ ط¯ط±ط³طھ ظ†غŒط³طھ.', 403)
    _fails.pop(('pw', current_user.id), None)
    return None


def is_owner():
    """ظ…ط§ظ„ع© ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ = ظ‚ط¯غŒظ…غŒâ€Œطھط±غŒظ† ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±ط› ظپظ‚ط· ط§ظˆ ط¨ظ‡ ظ¾ط´طھغŒط¨ط§ظ† ظˆ ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ ع©ظ„ ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯."""
    first = db.session.query(func.min(User.id)).scalar()
    return current_user.is_authenticated and current_user.id == first


def _jdate(dt):
    return jdatetime.date.fromgregorian(date=dt.date()).strftime('%Y/%m/%d')


@bp.record_once
def _register_cli(state):
    for cmd in (reset_password, make_license, list_licenses, restore_cmd):
        state.app.cli.add_command(cmd)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- طµظپط­ظ‡
@bp.route('/account')
def account_page():
    now = datetime.now()
    plan = current_user.effective_plan
    exp = current_user.plan_expires_at
    info = {'plan': plan, 'lifetime': plan == 'pro' and exp is None, 'expires': None, 'days_left': None, 'expired': False}
    if plan == 'pro' and exp:
        info['expires'] = _jdate(exp)
        info['days_left'] = max((exp - now).days, 0)
    elif current_user.plan == 'pro' and exp and exp < now:
        info['expired'] = True
        info['expires'] = _jdate(exp)
    return render_template('account.html', workshops=Workshop.query.order_by(Workshop.id).all(),
                           used=ArchivePhoto.query.count() + FinanceFile.query.count(), limit=current_user.photo_limit, info=info, owner=is_owner())


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ظ…ط´ط®طµط§طھ ظˆ ط±ظ…ط²
@bp.route('/api/account/profile', methods=['PUT'])
def update_profile():
    d = _body()
    err = _need_password(d)
    if err:
        return err
    name = (d.get('name') or '').strip()
    email = (d.get('email') or '').strip().lower() or None
    if not name:
        return _bad('ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    if len(name) > 100:
        return _bad('ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ظ†ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط¨غŒط´ ط§ط² غ±غ°غ° ط­ط±ظپ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    if '@' in name and name != current_user.name:
        return _bad('ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ظ†ط¨ط§غŒط¯ @ ط¯ط§ط´طھظ‡ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    if email and not EMAIL_RE.match(email):
        return _bad('ط§غŒظ…غŒظ„ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    if User.query.filter(User.id != current_user.id, func.lower(User.name) == name.lower()).first():
        return _bad('ط§غŒظ† ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.')
    if email and User.query.filter(User.id != current_user.id, func.lower(User.email) == email).first():
        return _bad('ط§غŒظ† ط§غŒظ…غŒظ„ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.')
    if email != current_user.email:
        current_user.email_verified = False
    current_user.name, current_user.email = name, email
    db.session.commit()
    return _ok()


@bp.route('/api/account/password', methods=['PUT'])
def change_password():
    d = _body()
    err = _need_password(d)
    if err:
        return err
    new = d.get('new_password') or ''
    if len(new) < 6:
        return _bad('ط±ظ…ط² ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط­ط¯ط§ظ‚ظ„ غ¶ ع©ط§ط±ط§ع©طھط± ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    if new != d.get('repeat_password'):
        return _bad('طھع©ط±ط§ط± ط±ظ…ط² ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ غŒع©ط³ط§ظ† ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    if new == (d.get('current_password') or ''):
        return _bad('ط±ظ…ط² ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط¨ط§ ط±ظ…ط² ظپط¹ظ„غŒ ظپط±ظ‚ ط¯ط§ط´طھظ‡ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    current_user.set_password(new)
    db.session.commit()
    return _ok()

@bp.route('/api/workshop/<int:workshop_id>', methods=['PUT'])
def rename_workshop(workshop_id):
    w = db.session.get(Workshop, workshop_id)
    if not w:
        return _bad('ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    name = (_body().get('name') or '').strip()
    if not name:
        return _bad('ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    if len(name) > 100:
        return _bad('ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ظ†ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط¨غŒط´ ط§ط² غ±غ°غ° ط­ط±ظپ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    w.name = name
    db.session.commit()
    return _ok()


@bp.route('/api/workshop/<int:workshop_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
def delete_workshop(workshop_id):
    w = db.session.get(Workshop, workshop_id)
    if not w:
        return _bad('ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    err = _need_password(_body())          # ط­ط°ظپ ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ظ‡ظ…غŒط´ظ‡ ط±ظ…ط² ط¹ط¨ظˆط± ظ…غŒâ€Œط®ظˆط§ظ‡ط¯
    if err:
        return err
    db.session.delete(w)
    db.session.commit()
    return _ok()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ظ„ط§غŒط³ظ†ط³ ظˆ ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط­ط±ظپظ‡â€Œط§غŒ
def _norm_code(s):
    n = re.sub(r'[^A-Z0-9]', '', (s or '').upper())
    return 'KRG' + n if n and not n.startswith('KRG') and len(n) == 12 else n


def _hash_code(norm):
    return hashlib.sha256(norm.encode()).hexdigest()


def new_license_code():
    body = ''.join(secrets.choice(ALPHABET) for _ in range(12))
    return 'KRG-%s-%s-%s' % (body[:4], body[4:8], body[8:])


@bp.route('/api/account/license', methods=['POST'])
def redeem_license():
    if _throttled('lic'):
        return _bad('طھظ„ط§ط´ ظ†ط§ظ…ظˆظپظ‚ ط²غŒط§ط¯ ط¨ظˆط¯ط› ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط¯ظˆط¨ط§ط±ظ‡ ط§ظ…طھط­ط§ظ† ع©ظ†غŒط¯.', 429)
    norm = _norm_code(_body().get('code'))
    lic = License.query.filter_by(code_hash=_hash_code(norm)).first() if norm else None
    if not lic or lic.used_by:
        _record_fail('lic')
        return _bad('ع©ط¯ ظ„ط§غŒط³ظ†ط³ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ غŒط§ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.')
    user, now = current_user, datetime.now()
    if user.plan == 'pro' and user.plan_expires_at is None:
        return _bad('ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط­ط±ظپظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ط´ظ…ط§ ط¯ط§ط¦ظ…غŒ ط§ط³طھ ظˆ ط¨ظ‡ ظ„ط§غŒط³ظ†ط³ ظ†غŒط§ط²غŒ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.')
    claimed = License.query.filter_by(id=lic.id, used_by=None).update({'used_by': user.id, 'used_at': now})
    if not claimed:
        db.session.rollback()
        return _bad('ع©ط¯ ظ„ط§غŒط³ظ†ط³ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ غŒط§ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.')
    start = user.plan_expires_at if (user.plan == 'pro' and user.plan_expires_at and user.plan_expires_at > now) else now
    user.plan = 'pro'
    user.plan_expires_at = start + timedelta(days=lic.days)
    db.session.commit()
    _fails.pop(('lic', user.id), None)
    return _ok(expires=_jdate(user.plan_expires_at), days=lic.days)


@click.command('make-license')
@click.option('--days', type=int, default=365, show_default=True, help='ظ…ط¯طھ ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط­ط±ظپظ‡â€Œط§غŒ (ط±ظˆط²)')
@click.option('--count', type=int, default=1, show_default=True, help='طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ع©ط¯')
@click.option('--note', default='', help='غŒط§ط¯ط¯ط§ط´طھ (ظ…ط«ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط±غŒط¯ط§ط±)')
@with_appcontext
def make_license(days, count, note):
    """ط³ط§ط®طھ ع©ط¯ ظ„ط§غŒط³ظ†ط³ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط±ظ…طµط±ظپ:  flask --app app make-license --days 365 --count 5"""
    if days < 1 or not 1 <= count <= 200:
        raise click.ClickException('days ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ظ…ط«ط¨طھ ظˆ count ط¨غŒظ† غ± طھط§ غ²غ°غ° ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    codes = []
    for _ in range(count):
        code = new_license_code()
        db.session.add(License(code_hash=_hash_code(_norm_code(code)), hint=code[-4:], days=days, note=note[:100]))
        codes.append(code)
    db.session.commit()
    click.echo('ع©ط¯ظ‡ط§ (ظپظ‚ط· ظ‡ظ…غŒظ† غŒع© ط¨ط§ط± ظ†ظ…ط§غŒط´ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆظ†ط¯ط› ط¬ط§غŒغŒ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ع©ظ†غŒط¯):')
    for c in codes:
        click.echo('  ' + c)


@click.command('list-licenses')
@with_appcontext
def list_licenses():
    """ظپظ‡ط±ط³طھ ظ„ط§غŒط³ظ†ط³â€Œظ‡ط§ ظˆ ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ظ…طµط±ظپط´ط§ظ†."""
    rows = License.query.order_by(License.id).all()
    if not rows:
        click.echo('ظ‡ظ†ظˆط² ظ„ط§غŒط³ظ†ط³غŒ ط³ط§ط®طھظ‡ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.')
    for r in rows:
        who = ''
        if r.used_by:
            u = db.session.get(User, r.used_by)
            who = '  â†گ ظ…طµط±ظپâ€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ طھظˆط³ط· آ«%sآ» ط¯ط± %s' % (u.name if u else r.used_by, _jdate(r.used_at))
        click.echo('#%d  ...%s  %d ط±ظˆط²  %s%s' % (r.id, r.hint, r.days, r.note or '', who or '  (ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡)'))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ظ¾ط´طھغŒط¨ط§ظ†â€Œع¯غŒط±غŒ
def _write_backup_zip(dest):
    """غŒع© zip ط´ط§ظ…ظ„ ظ†ط³ط®ظ‡â€ŒغŒ غŒع©ط¯ط³طھظگ ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³ ظˆ ظ‡ظ…ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ط¹ع©ط³â€Œظ‡ط§ ط¯ط± ظ…ط³غŒط± dest ظ…غŒâ€Œط³ط§ط²ط¯."""
    db_path = db.engine.url.database
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        snap = os.path.join(td, 'database.db')
        src, dst = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=30), sqlite3.connect(snap)
        try:
            src.backup(dst)
        finally:
            dst.close(); src.close()
        uploads = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(db_path), 'uploads')
        with zipfile.ZipFile(dest, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
            z.write(snap, 'database.db')
            for folder, _dirs, files in os.walk(uploads):
                for fn in files:
                    full = os.path.join(folder, fn)
                    z.write(full, os.path.join('uploads', os.path.relpath(full, uploads)))


@bp.route('/account/backup.zip')
def backup():
    if not is_owner():
        return _bad('ظپظ‚ط· ظ…ط§ظ„ع© ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ط¨ظ‡ ظ¾ط´طھغŒط¨ط§ظ† ع©ط§ظ…ظ„ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.', 403)
    tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.zip', delete=False)
    tmp.close()
    _write_backup_zip(tmp.name)

    @after_this_request
    def _cleanup(resp):
        try:
            os.remove(tmp.name)
        except OSError:
            pass
        return resp

    stamp = jdatetime.date.today().strftime('%Y%m%d')
    return send_file(tmp.name, as_attachment=True, download_name='kargah-backup-%s.zip' % stamp, mimetype='application/zip')


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ
class RestoreError(Exception):
    pass


def _validate_snapshot(path):
    try:
        cn = sqlite3.connect('file:%s?mode=ro' % path.replace('\\', '/'), uri=True)
        try:
            if cn.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0] != 'ok':
                raise RestoreError('ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³ ط¯ط§ط®ظ„ ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ط³ط§ظ„ظ… ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
            tables = {r[0] for r in cn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
            if not REQUIRED_TABLES <= tables:
                raise RestoreError('ط§غŒظ† ظپط§غŒظ„طŒ ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ط§غŒظ† ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظ†غŒط³طھ (ط¬ط¯ظˆظ„â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ„ط§ط²ظ… ط±ط§ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯).')
            if cn.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users').fetchone()[0] < 1:
                raise RestoreError('ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ظ‡غŒع† ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯ط› ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط§ط² ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œطھظˆط§ظ†ط³طھغŒط¯ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ط´ظˆغŒط¯.')
        finally:
            cn.close()
    except sqlite3.DatabaseError:
        raise RestoreError('ظپط§غŒظ„ ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³ ط¯ط§ط®ظ„ ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ط®ط±ط§ط¨ ط§ط³طھ.')


def restore_zip(fileobj):
    """ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ط±ط§ ط§ط¹طھط¨ط§ط±ط³ظ†ط¬غŒ ظ…غŒâ€Œع©ظ†ط¯طŒ ط§ط² ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ظپط¹ظ„غŒ غŒع© ظ†ط³ط®ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ط§غŒظ…ظ†غŒ ظ…غŒâ€Œع¯غŒط±ط¯ ظˆ ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط¬ط§غŒع¯ط²غŒظ† ظ…غŒâ€Œع©ظ†ط¯."""
    try:
        z = zipfile.ZipFile(fileobj)
    except zipfile.BadZipFile:
        raise RestoreError('ظپط§غŒظ„ zip ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    names = z.namelist()
    if 'database.db' not in names:
        raise RestoreError('ط¯ط§ط®ظ„ zip ظپط§غŒظ„ database.db ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.')
    if sum(i.file_size for i in z.infolist()) > MAX_UNPACKED:
        raise RestoreError('ط­ط¬ظ… ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ط¨غŒط´ ط§ط² ط­ط¯ ظ…ط¬ط§ط² ط§ط³طھ.')
    db_path = db.engine.url.database
    inst = os.path.dirname(db_path)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
        snap = os.path.join(td, 'database.db')
        with z.open('database.db') as s, open(snap, 'wb') as d:
            shutil.copyfileobj(s, d)
        _validate_snapshot(snap)
        stage = os.path.join(td, 'uploads')
        os.makedirs(stage)
        for n in names:
            if not n.startswith('uploads/') or n.endswith('/'):
                continue
            dest = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(stage, n[len('uploads/'):]))
            if not dest.startswith(os.path.normpath(stage) + os.sep):
                continue                                   # ظ…ط³غŒط± ظ…ط´ع©ظˆع© (path traversal) ظ†ط§ط¯غŒط¯ظ‡ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True)
            with z.open(n) as s, open(dest, 'wb') as d:
                shutil.copyfileobj(s, d)

        os.makedirs(os.path.join(inst, 'backups'), exist_ok=True)
        safety = os.path.join(inst, 'backups', 'before-restore-%s.zip' % datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-%f'))
        _write_backup_zip(safety)

        db.session.remove()
        src, dst = sqlite3.connect(snap), sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=30)
        try:
            src.backup(dst)
        finally:
            dst.close(); src.close()
        uploads = os.path.join(inst, 'uploads')
        shutil.rmtree(uploads, ignore_errors=True)
        shutil.copytree(stage, uploads)
    db.engine.dispose()
    db.create_all()
    ensure_columns()
    seed_missing_defaults()
    adopt_orphans()
    return safety


@bp.route('/api/account/restore', methods=['POST'])
def restore_api():
    if not is_owner():
        return _bad('ظپظ‚ط· ظ…ط§ظ„ع© ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œطھظˆط§ظ†ط¯ ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ط±ط§ ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ ع©ظ†ط¯.', 403)
    err = _need_password(request.form)
    if err:
        return err
    if (request.form.get('confirm') or '').strip() != 'ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ':
        return _bad('ط¨ط±ط§غŒ طھط£غŒغŒط¯طŒ ع©ظ„ظ…ظ‡â€ŒغŒ آ«ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒآ» ط±ط§ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ط§ظ‹ ط¨ظ†ظˆغŒط³غŒط¯.')
    f = request.files.get('file')
    if not f or not f.filename:
        return _bad('ظپط§غŒظ„ ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ (zip) ط±ط§ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    try:
        safety = restore_zip(f.stream)
    except RestoreError as e:
        return _bad(str(e))
    return _ok(safety=os.path.basename(safety))


@click.command('restore')
@click.argument('zipfile_path', type=click.Path(exists=True, dir_okay=False))
@click.option('--yes', is_flag=True, help='ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ظ¾ط±ط³ط´ طھط£غŒغŒط¯')
@with_appcontext
def restore_cmd(zipfile_path, yes):
    """ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾:  flask --app app restore backup.zip"""
    if not yes:
        click.confirm('ظ‡ظ…ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ظپط¹ظ„غŒ ط¨ط§ ط¨ع©ط§ظ¾ ط¬ط§غŒع¯ط²غŒظ† ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯ (ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط´ غŒع© ظ†ط³ط®ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ط§غŒظ…ظ†غŒ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯). ط§ط¯ط§ظ…ظ‡طں', abort=True)
    try:
        with open(zipfile_path, 'rb') as f:
            safety = restore_zip(f)
    except RestoreError as e:
        raise click.ClickException(str(e))
    click.echo('ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯. ظ†ط³ط®ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ط§غŒظ…ظ†غŒظگ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ط§ط² ط¢ظ†: ' + safety)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ ط±ظ…ط²
@click.command('reset-password')
@click.option('--user', 'ident', default=None, help='ظ†ط§ظ… غŒط§ ط§غŒظ…غŒظ„ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± (ط§ع¯ط± ظپظ‚ط· غŒع© ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ظ‡ط³طھ ظ„ط§ط²ظ… ظ†غŒط³طھ)')
@click.password_option('--password', prompt='ط±ظ…ط² ط¬ط¯غŒط¯', confirmation_prompt=True, help='ط±ظ…ط² ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ (ط­ط¯ط§ظ‚ظ„ غ¶ ع©ط§ط±ط§ع©طھط±)')
@with_appcontext
def reset_password(ident, password):
    """ط¨ط§ط²غŒط§ط¨غŒ ط±ظ…ط² ظپط±ط§ظ…ظˆط´â€Œط´ط¯ظ‡:  flask --app app reset-password"""
    if len(password) < 6:
        raise click.ClickException('ط±ظ…ط² ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط­ط¯ط§ظ‚ظ„ غ¶ ع©ط§ط±ط§ع©طھط± ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    if ident:
        user = (User.query.filter(func.lower(User.email) == ident.lower()).first()
                or User.query.filter_by(name=ident).first())
    else:
        users = User.query.all()
        user = users[0] if len(users) == 1 else None
    if not user:
        raise click.ClickException('ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ظ…ط´ط®طµ ظ†غŒط³طھط› ط§ط² --user ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    user.set_password(password)
    db.session.commit()
    click.echo('ط±ظ…ط² آ«%sآ» ط¹ظˆط¶ ط´ط¯.' % user.name)

# D:\kargah\app.py

from flask import Flask, render_template, request, redirect, url_for, session, jsonify, abort

from dotenv import load_dotenv
from extensions import db, login_manager
import secrets
from datetime import datetime
import jdatetime
import os

app = Flask(__name__)

# طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI'] = 'sqlite:///database.db'
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS'] = False
load_dotenv()


def _ensure_secret_key():
    """SECRET_KEY ط±ط§ ط§ط² .env ظ…غŒâ€Œط®ظˆط§ظ†ط¯ط› ط§ع¯ط± ظ†ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ…غŒâ€Œط³ط§ط²ط¯ ظˆ ط¯ط± .env ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œع©ظ†ط¯."""
    key = os.environ.get('SECRET_KEY')
    if key:
        return key
    key = secrets.token_hex(32)
    env_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), '.env')
    with open(env_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write('\nSECRET_KEY=%s\n' % key)
    return key


app.config['SECRET_KEY'] = _ensure_secret_key()
app.config['SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY'] = True
app.config['SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE'] = 'Lax'

db.init_app(app)
login_manager.init_app(app)
login_manager.login_view = 'auth.login'

# ==================== ظ…ط¯ظ„â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³ ====================

from models import (Workshop, FarmingYear, Activity, Material, DailyWork, WorkMaterial,
                    ensure_columns, seed_missing_defaults)

# ==================== طھظˆط§ط¨ط¹ ع©ظ…ع©غŒ ====================
def get_today_shamsi():
    now = jdatetime.datetime.now()
    return now.strftime("%Y/%m/%d")

# ==================== Routes ط§طµظ„غŒ ====================
@app.route('/')
def index():
    """طµظپط­ظ‡ ط§طµظ„غŒ ظ„غŒط³طھ ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§"""
    workshops = Workshop.query.all()
    return render_template('index.html', workshops=workshops)

@app.route('/workshop/<int:workshop_id>/<int:year_id>')
def workshop_dashboard(workshop_id, year_id):
    """ط¯ط§ط´ط¨ظˆط±ط¯ غŒع© ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ط®ط§طµ ط¨ط§ ط³ط§ظ„ ع©ط§ط±غŒ ظ…ط´ط®طµ"""
    workshop = db.get_or_404(Workshop, workshop_id)
    farming_year = db.get_or_404(FarmingYear, year_id)
    if farming_year.workshop_id != workshop.id:
        abort(404)
    all_years = FarmingYear.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop_id).all()
    
    today = get_today_shamsi()
    activities = Activity.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop_id, is_active=True).all()
    works = DailyWork.query.filter_by(farming_year_id=year_id, is_temp=False).order_by(DailyWork.id.desc()).all()
    materials = Material.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop_id).all()
    
    return render_template('workshop.html', 
                         workshop=workshop,
                         farming_year=farming_year,
                         all_years=all_years,
                         today=today, 
                         activities=activities, 
                         works=works,
                         materials=materials)

# ==================== [patch4] ط§غŒظ…ظ†غŒطŒ ط§ط¹طھط¨ط§ط±ط³ظ†ط¬غŒ ظˆ APIظ‡ط§غŒ ط§طµظ„ط§ط­â€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ====================
# [patch4]
import functools
import re
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from sqlalchemy import event
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine
from werkzeug.exceptions import HTTPException

from models import Income, Expense, ArchiveEntry
from finance import _date as _fdate, _num as _fnum

app.config['REMEMBER_COOKIE_HTTPONLY'] = True
app.config['REMEMBER_COOKIE_SAMESITE'] = 'Lax'
if os.environ.get('COOKIE_SECURE') == '1':          # ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط§ط² ط±ظپطھظ† ط±ظˆغŒ HTTPS
    app.config['SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE'] = True
    app.config['REMEMBER_COOKIE_SECURE'] = True
if os.environ.get('BEHIND_PROXY') == '1':           # ظ¾ط´طھ nginx
    from werkzeug.middleware.proxy_fix import ProxyFix
    app.wsgi_app = ProxyFix(app.wsgi_app, x_for=1, x_proto=1, x_host=1)


@event.listens_for(Engine, 'connect')
def _sqlite_foreign_keys_on(dbapi_conn, _record):
    """SQLite ط¨ظ‡â€Œط·ظˆط± ظ¾غŒط´â€Œظپط±ط¶ ع©ظ„غŒط¯ ط®ط§ط±ط¬غŒ ط±ط§ ط§ط¬ط±ط§ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œع©ظ†ط¯."""
    if dbapi_conn.__class__.__module__.split('.')[0] == 'sqlite3':
        cur = dbapi_conn.cursor()
        cur.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON')
        cur.close()


# ---------- ظ…ط­ط§ظپط¸ CSRF (ط¨ط±ط±ط³غŒ ظ…ط¨ط¯ط£ ط¯ط±ط®ظˆط§ط³طھâ€Œظ‡ط§غŒ طھط؛غŒغŒط±ط¯ظ‡ظ†ط¯ظ‡) ----------
_UNSAFE_METHODS = {'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'}
_EXTRA_HOSTS = {h.strip() for h in os.environ.get('ALLOWED_ORIGINS', '').split(',') if h.strip()}


@app.before_request
def _origin_guard():
    if request.method not in _UNSAFE_METHODS:
        return None
    src = request.headers.get('Origin') or request.headers.get('Referer') or ''
    src_host = urlparse(src).netloc if src else ''
    if src_host and src_host in _EXTRA_HOSTS:
        return None
    site = request.headers.get('Sec-Fetch-Site')
    if site in ('same-origin', 'none'):
        return None
    if site is None and (not src or src_host == request.host):
        return None
    msg = 'ط¯ط±ط®ظˆط§ط³طھ ط§ط² ظ…ط¨ط¯ط£ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط±ط¯ ط´ط¯.'
    if request.path.startswith('/api/'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': msg}), 403
    return msg, 403


# ---------- ط§ط¨ط²ط§ط±ظ‡ط§غŒ ظ…ط´طھط±ع© APIظ‡ط§ ----------
class _NotFound(LookupError):
    pass


def _api(fn):
    """ط®ط·ط§ظ‡ط§ ط±ط§ ط¨ظ‡ JSON ظپط§ط±ط³غŒ طھط¨ط¯غŒظ„ ظ…غŒâ€Œع©ظ†ط¯ ظˆ ظ…طھظ† ط®ط·ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط®ظ„غŒ ط±ط§ ظ„ظˆ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œط¯ظ‡ط¯."""
    @functools.wraps(fn)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        try:
            return fn(*args, **kwargs)
        except HTTPException:
            raise
        except _NotFound as e:
            db.session.rollback()
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 404
        except ValueError as e:
            db.session.rollback()
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 400
        except Exception:
            db.session.rollback()
            app.logger.exception('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± %s', fn.__name__)
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'ط®ط·ط§غŒ ط¯ط§ط®ظ„غŒ ط³ط±ظˆط±ط› ط¯ظˆط¨ط§ط±ظ‡ طھظ„ط§ط´ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.'}), 500
    return wrapper


def _body():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise ValueError('ط§ط·ظ„ط§ط¹ط§طھ ط§ط±ط³ط§ظ„â€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    return data


def _need_id(value, label):
    n = _fnum(value)
    if not n:
        raise ValueError('%s ظ…ط´ط®طµ ظ†غŒط³طھ.' % label)
    return n


def _get(model, ident, label):
    obj = db.session.get(model, ident)
    if obj is None:
        raise _NotFound('%s ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.' % label)
    return obj


def _text(value, label, max_len, required=True):
    s = str(value).strip() if value is not None else ''
    if required and not s:
        raise ValueError('%s ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.' % label)
    if len(s) > max_len:
        raise ValueError('%s ظ†ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط¨غŒط´ ط§ط² %d ط­ط±ظپ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.' % (label, max_len))
    return s


def _bool(v):
    if isinstance(v, str):
        return v.strip().lower() in ('1', 'true', 'on', 'yes')
    return bool(v)


def _work_values(data, year):
    """ظ…ظ‚ط§ط¯غŒط± ط§ط¹طھط¨ط§ط±ط³ظ†ط¬غŒâ€Œط´ط¯ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ع©ط§ط± ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡ط› طھط§ط±غŒط® ظ‡ظ…غŒط´ظ‡ ط¨ظ‡ YYYY/MM/DD طµظپط±â€Œط¯ط§ط± طھط¨ط¯غŒظ„ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯."""
    act = _get(Activity, _need_id(data.get('activity_id'), 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ'), 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ')
    if act.workshop_id != year.workshop_id:
        raise ValueError('ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨â€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    return dict(
        date_shamsi=_fdate(data.get('date')),
        activity_id=act.id,
        worker_count=_fnum(data.get('worker_count')) or 0,
        total_wage=_fnum(data.get('total_wage')) or 0,
        food_cost=_fnum(data.get('food_cost')) or 0,
        description=_text(data.get('description'), 'طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ', 2000, required=False),
    )


def _normalize_old_dates():
    """طھط§ط±غŒط®â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ‚ط¯غŒظ…غŒظگ ع©ط§ط± ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡ (ط§ط±ظ‚ط§ظ… ظپط§ط±ط³غŒ / ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† طµظپط± ط§ط¨طھط¯ط§غŒغŒ) ط±ط§ غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± ط§طµظ„ط§ط­ ظ…غŒâ€Œع©ظ†ط¯."""
    fixed = bad = 0
    for wid, d in db.session.query(DailyWork.id, DailyWork.date_shamsi).all():
        if d and re.match(r'^[0-9]{4}/[0-9]{2}/[0-9]{2}$', d):
            continue
        try:
            n = _fdate(d)
        except ValueError:
            bad += 1
            continue
        DailyWork.query.filter_by(id=wid).update({'date_shamsi': n})
        fixed += 1
    if fixed:
        db.session.commit()
    if fixed or bad:
        print('[patch4] طھط§ط±غŒط® ع©ط§ط± ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡: %d ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ط§طµظ„ط§ط­ ط´ط¯طŒ %d ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ…ط§ظ†ط¯.' % (fixed, bad))


# ==================== APIظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ====================
@app.route('/api/create_workshop', methods=['POST'])
@_api
def create_workshop():
    """ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ ط¨ط§ ع†ظ†ط¯ ط³ط§ظ„ ع©ط§ط±غŒ"""
    data = _body()
    name = _text(data.get('name'), 'ظ†ط§ظ… ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡', 100)
    raw_years = data.get('years')
    if not isinstance(raw_years, list):
        raise ValueError('ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    years = []
    for y in raw_years:
        y = _text(y, 'ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ', 10, required=False)
        if y and y not in years:
            years.append(y)
    if not years:
        raise ValueError('ط­ط¯ط§ظ‚ظ„ غŒع© ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')

    workshop = Workshop(name=name)
    db.session.add(workshop)
    db.session.flush()

    first_year_id = None
    for year_str in years:
        year = FarmingYear(year=year_str, workshop_id=workshop.id)
        db.session.add(year)
        db.session.flush()
        if first_year_id is None:
            first_year_id = year.id

    for n, has_mat in [('ظ‡ط±ط³', False), ('ظ…ط­ظ„ظˆظ„â€Œظ¾ط§ط´غŒ', True), ('ط¢ط¨غŒط§ط±غŒ', False),
                       ('ط³ظ…â€Œظ¾ط§ط´غŒ', True), ('ع©ظˆط¯ط¯ظ‡غŒ', True), ('طھط²ط±غŒظ‚ ع©ظˆط¯', True)]:
        db.session.add(Activity(name=n, has_material=has_mat, workshop_id=workshop.id))

    for n, t, u in [('ع©ظˆط¯ ط§ظˆط±ظ‡', 'ع©ظˆط¯', 'ع©غŒظ„ظˆع¯ط±ظ…'), ('ع©ظˆط¯ ظ¾طھط§ط³', 'ع©ظˆط¯', 'ع©غŒظ„ظˆع¯ط±ظ…'),
                    ('ع©ظˆط¯ ظ…ط§غŒط¹', 'ع©ظˆط¯ ظ…ط§غŒط¹', 'ظ„غŒطھط±'), ('ط³ظ… ظ‚ط§ط±ع†â€Œع©ط´', 'ط³ظ…', 'ظ„غŒطھط±'),
                    ('ط³ظ… ط­ط´ط±ظ‡â€Œع©ط´', 'ط³ظ…', 'ظ„غŒطھط±')]:
        db.session.add(Material(name=n, type=t, unit=u, workshop_id=workshop.id))

    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'workshop_id': workshop.id, 'first_year_id': first_year_id})


@app.route('/api/add_farming_year', methods=['POST'])
@_api
def add_farming_year():
    """ط§ط¶ط§ظپظ‡ ع©ط±ط¯ظ† ط³ط§ظ„ ع©ط§ط±غŒ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯"""
    data = _body()
    workshop = _get(Workshop, _need_id(data.get('workshop_id'), 'ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡'), 'ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡')
    name = _text(data.get('year'), 'ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ', 10)
    if FarmingYear.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop.id, year=name).first():
        raise ValueError('ط§غŒظ† ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.')
    year = FarmingYear(year=name, workshop_id=workshop.id)
    db.session.add(year)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'year_id': year.id})


@app.route('/api/delete_year/<int:year_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@_api
def delete_year(year_id):
    """ط­ط°ظپ ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒط› ط§ع¯ط± ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯ ظپظ‚ط· ط¨ط§ confirm=1 (طھط£غŒغŒط¯ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±) ط­ط°ظپ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯."""
    year = _get(FarmingYear, year_id, 'ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ')
    counts = [('ع©ط§ط± ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡', DailyWork.query.filter_by(farming_year_id=year.id).count()),
              ('ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯', Income.query.filter_by(farming_year_id=year.id).count()),
              ('ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡', Expense.query.filter_by(farming_year_id=year.id).count()),
              ('ط¢ط±ط´غŒظˆ', ArchiveEntry.query.filter_by(farming_year_id=year.id).count())]
    total = sum(c for _, c in counts)
    if total and request.args.get('confirm') != '1':
        detail = 'طŒ '.join('%d %s' % (c, n) for n, c in counts if c)
        raise ValueError('ط§غŒظ† ط³ط§ظ„ ط´ط§ظ…ظ„ %s ط§ط³طھط› ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ظ‡ط§غŒغŒ ط¨ط§غŒط¯ طھط£غŒغŒط¯ ط´ظˆط¯.' % detail)
    db.session.delete(year)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


# ==================== APIظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط§ط± ====================
def _new_work(is_temp):
    data = _body()
    year = _get(FarmingYear, _need_id(data.get('farming_year_id'), 'ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ'), 'ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ')
    work = DailyWork(farming_year_id=year.id, is_temp=is_temp, **_work_values(data, year))
    db.session.add(work)
    db.session.commit()
    return work


@app.route('/api/save_work_temp', methods=['POST'])
@_api
def save_work_temp():
    """ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…ظˆظ‚طھ ع©ط§ط± ط¬ط¯غŒط¯ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ط§ط² ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯"""
    work = _new_work(True)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'work_id': work.id})


@app.route('/api/save_work_final', methods=['POST'])
@_api
def save_work_final():
    """ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ†ظ‡ط§غŒغŒ ع©ط§ط± ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ظ…ظˆط§ط¯"""
    _new_work(False)
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@app.route('/api/save_material_usage', methods=['POST'])
@_api
def save_material_usage():
    """ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯ ظ…طµط±ظپغŒ غŒع© ع©ط§ط± ظˆ ظ†ظ‡ط§غŒغŒâ€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ط¢ظ†"""
    data = _body()
    work = _get(DailyWork, _need_id(data.get('work_id'), 'ع©ط§ط±'), 'ع©ط§ط±')
    year = db.session.get(FarmingYear, work.farming_year_id)
    items = data.get('materials')
    if not isinstance(items, list):
        raise ValueError('ظپظ‡ط±ط³طھ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')

    # ظ‚غŒظ…طھظگ ظ„ط­ط¸ظ‡ ط«ط¨طھ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯غŒ ع©ظ‡ ط§ط² ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ط¯ط± ط§غŒظ† ع©ط§ط± ط¨ظˆط¯ظ‡â€Œط§ظ†ط¯ ط­ظپط¸ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯ (ظ‡ظ†ع¯ط§ظ… ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´)
    old_prices = {m.material_id: m.unit_price for m in WorkMaterial.query.filter_by(work_id=work.id)}
    rows = []
    for it in items:
        if not isinstance(it, dict):
            raise ValueError('ظپظ‡ط±ط³طھ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
        mat = _get(Material, _need_id(it.get('material_id'), 'ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡'), 'ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡')
        if mat.workshop_id != year.workshop_id:
            raise ValueError('ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨â€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
        qty = _fnum(it.get('quantity'), False) or 0.0
        # ظ‚غŒظ…طھ طµظپط±/ط®ط§ظ„غŒ ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯ طھط§ ط¨ط§ طھط¹غŒغŒظ† ظ‚غŒظ…طھ ط¨ط¹ط¯غŒطŒ ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡ ظ…ط­ط§ط³ط¨ظ‡ ط´ظˆط¯
        price = old_prices[mat.id] if mat.id in old_prices else (mat.default_price or None)
        rows.append(WorkMaterial(work_id=work.id, material_id=mat.id, quantity=qty, unit_price=price))

    WorkMaterial.query.filter_by(work_id=work.id).delete()
    db.session.add_all(rows)
    work.is_temp = False
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})

    db.session.commit()

@app.route('/api/add_activity', methods=['POST'])
@_api
def add_activity():
    """ط§ط¶ط§ظپظ‡ ع©ط±ط¯ظ† ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯"""
    data = _body()
    workshop = _get(Workshop, _need_id(data.get('workshop_id'), 'ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡'), 'ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡')
    name = _text(data.get('name'), 'ظ†ط§ظ… ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ', 100)
    if Activity.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop.id, name=name, is_active=True).first():
        raise ValueError('ط§غŒظ† ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.')
    activity = Activity(name=name, has_material=_bool(data.get('has_material')), workshop_id=workshop.id)
    db.session.add(activity)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': activity.id, 'name': activity.name})


@app.route('/api/update_activity/<int:activity_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@_api
def update_activity(activity_id):
    """ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ"""
    data = _body()
    activity = _get(Activity, activity_id, 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ')
    name = _text(data.get('name'), 'ظ†ط§ظ… ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ', 100)
    clash = Activity.query.filter_by(workshop_id=activity.workshop_id, name=name, is_active=True).first()
    if clash and clash.id != activity.id:
        raise ValueError('ط§غŒظ† ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.')
    activity.name = name
    activity.has_material = _bool(data.get('has_material'))
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@app.route('/api/delete_activity/<int:activity_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@_api
def delete_activity(activity_id):
    """ط­ط°ظپ ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھط› ط§ع¯ط± ط¯ط± ع©ط§ط±ظ‡ط§غŒ ط«ط¨طھâ€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡طŒ ط¨ظ‡â€Œط¬ط§غŒ ط­ط°ظپ ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯."""
    activity = _get(Activity, activity_id, 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ')
    used = DailyWork.query.filter_by(activity_id=activity.id).count()
    if used:
        activity.is_active = False
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({'success': True, 'deactivated': True,
                        'message': 'ط§غŒظ† ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ ط¯ط± %d ع©ط§ط± ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡ط› ط¨ظ‡â€Œط¬ط§غŒ ط­ط°ظپطŒ ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ط´ط¯.' % used})
    db.session.delete(activity)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})

@app.route('/api/add_material', methods=['POST'])
@_api
def add_material():
    """ط§ط¶ط§ظپظ‡ ع©ط±ط¯ظ† ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯"""
    data = _body()
    workshop = _get(Workshop, _need_id(data.get('workshop_id'), 'ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡'), 'ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡')
    name = _text(data.get('name'), 'ظ†ط§ظ… ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡', 100)
    if Material.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop.id, name=name).first():
        raise ValueError('ط§غŒظ† ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.')
    material = Material(
        name=name,
        type=_text(data.get('type') or 'ع©ظˆط¯', 'ظ†ظˆط¹ ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡', 50),
        unit=_text(data.get('unit') or 'ظ„غŒطھط±', 'ظˆط§ط­ط¯', 20),
        default_price=_fnum(data.get('default_price')) or 0,
        workshop_id=workshop.id)
    db.session.add(material)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': material.id, 'name': material.name})


@app.route('/api/update_material/<int:material_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@_api
def update_material(material_id):
    """ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ (ظ†ط§ظ…طŒ ظ†ظˆط¹طŒ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظˆ ط¯ط± طµظˆط±طھ ط§ط±ط³ط§ظ„طŒ ظ‚غŒظ…طھ)"""
    data = _body()
    material = _get(Material, material_id, 'ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡')
    name = _text(data.get('name'), 'ظ†ط§ظ… ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡', 100)
    clash = Material.query.filter_by(workshop_id=material.workshop_id, name=name).first()
    if clash and clash.id != material.id:
        raise ValueError('ط§غŒظ† ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.')
    material.name = name
    material.type = _text(data.get('type') or material.type or 'ع©ظˆط¯', 'ظ†ظˆط¹ ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡', 50)
    material.unit = _text(data.get('unit') or material.unit or 'ظ„غŒطھط±', 'ظˆط§ط­ط¯', 20)
    if 'default_price' in data:
        material.default_price = _fnum(data.get('default_price')) or 0
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@app.route('/api/delete_material/<int:material_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@_api
def delete_material(material_id):
    """ط­ط°ظپ ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ (ظپط±ط§ظ†طھ ط§غŒظ† ظ…ط³غŒط± ط±ط§ طµط¯ط§ ظ…غŒâ€Œط²ط¯ ظˆظ„غŒ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ†ط¯ط§ط´طھ)ط› ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯."""
    material = _get(Material, material_id, 'ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡')
    used = WorkMaterial.query.filter_by(material_id=material.id).count()
    if used:
        raise ValueError('ط§غŒظ† ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط± %d ظ…طµط±ظپ ط«ط¨طھâ€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ظˆ ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†غŒط³طھ.' % used)
    from models import MaterialPurchase, StockAdjustment
    if (MaterialPurchase.query.filter_by(material_id=material.id).count()
            or StockAdjustment.query.filter_by(material_id=material.id).count()):
        raise ValueError('ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط§غŒظ† ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظپط§ع©طھظˆط± ط®ط±غŒط¯ غŒط§ طھظ†ط¸غŒظ… ط§ظ†ط¨ط§ط± ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ظˆ ظ‚ط§ط¨ظ„ ط­ط°ظپ ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    db.session.delete(material)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})

import os

from extensions import db

from finance import _date, _page_ctx, _err

bp = Blueprint('archive', __name__)

DEFAULT_STAGES = ['ط®ظˆط§ط¨ ط²ظ…ط³طھط§ظ†غŒ', 'ظ‡ط±ط³', 'ط¬ظˆط§ظ†ظ‡â€Œط²ظ†غŒ', 'ع¯ظ„â€Œط¯ظ‡غŒ', 'ط؛ظˆط±ظ‡', 'ط±ظ†ع¯â€Œع¯غŒط±غŒ',
                  'ط¨ط±ط¯ط§ط´طھ', 'ع©ط´ظ…ط´â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ', 'ط³ظ…â€Œظ¾ط§ط´غŒ', 'ط¢ط¨غŒط§ط±غŒ', 'ط¢ظپطھ ظˆ ط¨غŒظ…ط§ط±غŒ', 'ط³ط§غŒط±']
MAX_SIDE, THUMB_SIDE = 1600, 400


@bp.record_once
def _setup(state):
    state.app.config.setdefault('MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH', 64 * 1024 * 1024)  # ط­ط¯ط§ع©ط«ط± ط­ط¬ظ… ظ‡ط± ط¯ط±ط®ظˆط§ط³طھ
    state.app.cli.add_command(set_plan)


# ---------------- ظپط§غŒظ„â€Œظ‡ط§ ----------------
def _root():
    return os.path.join(current_app.instance_path, 'uploads')


def _path(rel):
    root = os.path.normpath(_root())
    p = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(root, rel))
    if not p.startswith(root + os.sep):
        abort(404)
    return p


def _save_image(storage, year_id):
    try:
        img = Image.open(storage.stream)
        img.load()
    except Exception:
        raise ValueError('غŒع©غŒ ط§ط² ظپط§غŒظ„â€Œظ‡ط§ ط¹ع©ط³ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(img)
    if img.mode != 'RGB':
        if 'A' in img.getbands() or img.mode == 'P':
            rgba = img.convert('RGBA')
            bg = Image.new('RGB', rgba.size, 'white')
            bg.paste(rgba, mask=rgba.split()[3])
            img = bg
        else:
            img = img.convert('RGB')
    big, thumb = img.copy(), img.copy()
    big.thumbnail((MAX_SIDE, MAX_SIDE))
    thumb.thumbnail((THUMB_SIDE, THUMB_SIDE))
    name = uuid.uuid4().hex
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(_root(), str(year_id)), exist_ok=True)
    rel, rel_t = '%s/%s.jpg' % (year_id, name), '%s/%s_t.jpg' % (year_id, name)
    big.save(_path(rel), 'JPEG', quality=82, optimize=True)
    thumb.save(_path(rel_t), 'JPEG', quality=75)
    return rel, rel_t, os.path.getsize(_path(rel))


def _remove_files(rels):
    for rel in rels:
        try:
            os.remove(_path(rel))
        except Exception:
            pass


@event.listens_for(ArchivePhoto, 'after_delete')
def _queue_files(mapper, connection, target):
    s = object_session(target)
    if s is not None:
        s.info.setdefault('trash', []).extend([target.filename, target.thumb])


@event.listens_for(Session, 'after_commit')
def _purge_files(session):
    _remove_files(session.info.pop('trash', []))


@event.listens_for(Session, 'after_rollback')
def _forget_files(session):
    session.info.pop('trash', None)


# ---------------- ظ¾ظ„ظ† / ط³ظ‚ظپ ط¹ع©ط³ ----------------
def _limit_error(adding, removing=0):
    limit = current_user.photo_limit
    if limit is None or adding <= 0:
        return None
    used = (ArchivePhoto.query.count() + _fin_count()) - removing
    if used + adding > limit:
        return ('ط³ظ‚ظپ ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط±ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ† %d ط¹ع©ط³ ط§ط³طھ ظˆ %d ط¬ط§غŒ ط®ط§ظ„غŒ ط¯ط§ط±غŒط¯. '
                'ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط¹ع©ط³ ظ†ط§ظ…ط­ط¯ظˆط¯طŒ ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط­ط±ظپظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ط±ط§ ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.' % (limit, max(limit - used, 0)))
    return None


@click.command('set-plan')
@click.argument('plan', type=click.Choice(['free', 'pro']))
@click.option('--user', 'ident', default=None, help='ظ†ط§ظ… غŒط§ ط§غŒظ…غŒظ„ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± (ط§ع¯ط± ظپظ‚ط· غŒع© ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ظ‡ط³طھ ظ„ط§ط²ظ… ظ†غŒط³طھ)')
@with_appcontext
def set_plan(plan, ident):
    """طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظ¾ظ„ظ† ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±:  flask --app app set-plan pro"""
    if ident:
        user = (User.query.filter(func.lower(User.email) == ident.lower()).first()
                or User.query.filter_by(name=ident).first())
    else:
        users = User.query.all()
        user = users[0] if len(users) == 1 else None
    if not user:
        raise click.ClickException('ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ظ…ط´ط®طµ ظ†غŒط³طھط› ط§ط² --user ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    user.plan = plan
    user.plan_expires_at = None   # [patch10] طھظ†ط¸غŒظ… ط¯ط³طھغŒ = ط¯ط§ط¦ظ…غŒ
    db.session.commit()
    click.echo('ظ¾ظ„ظ† %s ط±ظˆغŒ آ«%sآ» طھظ†ط¸غŒظ… ط´ط¯.' % (plan, user.name))


# ---------------- طµظپط­ظ‡ ----------------
@bp.route('/archive/<int:workshop_id>/<int:year_id>')
def archive_page(workshop_id, year_id):
    ctx = _page_ctx(workshop_id, year_id)
    stage = (request.args.get('stage') or '').strip()
    base = ArchiveEntry.query.filter_by(farming_year_id=year_id)
    used_stages = [s for (s,) in db.session.query(ArchiveEntry.stage)
                   .filter(ArchiveEntry.farming_year_id == year_id).distinct() if s]
    q = base.filter_by(stage=stage) if stage else base
    entries = q.order_by(ArchiveEntry.date_shamsi.desc(), ArchiveEntry.id.desc()).all()
    items = [{'id': e.id, 'title': e.title, 'stage': e.stage or '', 'date': e.date_shamsi,
              'description': e.description or '', 'photos': [p.id for p in e.photos]} for e in entries]
    return render_template(
        'archive.html', items=items, stage=stage, used_stages=used_stages,
        suggestions=list(dict.fromkeys(DEFAULT_STAGES + used_stages)),
        used=(ArchivePhoto.query.count() + _fin_count()), limit=current_user.photo_limit, plan=current_user.effective_plan, **ctx)


@bp.route('/media/<int:photo_id>')
def media(photo_id):
    return _send(photo_id, False)


@bp.route('/media/<int:photo_id>/thumb')
def media_thumb(photo_id):
    return _send(photo_id, True)


def _send(photo_id, thumb):
    p = db.session.get(ArchivePhoto, photo_id)
    if not p:
        abort(404)
    resp = send_file(_path(p.thumb if thumb else p.filename), mimetype='image/jpeg')
    resp.headers['Cache-Control'] = 'private, max-age=86400'
    return resp


# ---------------- API ----------------
def _int(v):
    try:
        return int(str(v).strip())
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0


def _fill(entry, f):
    entry.date_shamsi = _date(f.get('date'))
    title = (f.get('title') or '').strip()
    if not title:
        raise ValueError('ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    entry.title = title[:150]
    entry.stage = (f.get('stage') or '').strip()[:60]
    entry.description = (f.get('description') or '').strip()


def _uploads():
    return [x for x in request.files.getlist('photos') if x and x.filename]


def _store(files, year_id):
    saved = []
    try:
        for fs in files:
            saved.append(_save_image(fs, year_id))
    except ValueError:
        _remove_files([r for r, _, _ in saved] + [t for _, t, _ in saved])
        raise
    return saved


@bp.route('/api/archive/entries', methods=['POST'])
def api_create():
    year = db.session.get(FarmingYear, _int(request.form.get('year_id')))
    if not year:
        return _err('ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    entry = ArchiveEntry(farming_year_id=year.id)
    files = _uploads()
    try:
        _fill(entry, request.form)
        limit_msg = _limit_error(len(files))
        if limit_msg:
            return _err(limit_msg, 403)
        saved = _store(files, year.id)
    except ValueError as e:
        return _err(str(e))
    try:
        db.session.add(entry)
        for rel, rel_t, size in saved:
            entry.photos.append(ArchivePhoto(filename=rel, thumb=rel_t, size_bytes=size))
        db.session.commit()
    except Exception:
        db.session.rollback()
        _remove_files([r for r, _, _ in saved] + [t for _, t, _ in saved])
        return _err('ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 500)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': entry.id})


@bp.route('/api/archive/entries/<int:entry_id>', methods=['POST', 'DELETE'])
def api_entry(entry_id):
    entry = db.session.get(ArchiveEntry, entry_id)
    if not entry:
        return _err('ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    if request.method == 'DELETE':
        db.session.delete(entry)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    remove_ids = {_int(x) for x in (request.form.get('remove_ids') or '').split(',') if x.strip()}
    to_remove = [p for p in entry.photos if p.id in remove_ids]
    files = _uploads()
    try:
        _fill(entry, request.form)
        limit_msg = _limit_error(len(files), removing=len(to_remove))
        if limit_msg:
            db.session.rollback()
            return _err(limit_msg, 403)
        saved = _store(files, entry.farming_year_id)
    except ValueError as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return _err(str(e))
    try:
        for p in to_remove:
            db.session.delete(p)
        for rel, rel_t, size in saved:
            entry.photos.append(ArchivePhoto(filename=rel, thumb=rel_t, size_bytes=size))
        db.session.commit()
    except Exception:
        db.session.rollback()
        _remove_files([r for r, _, _ in saved] + [t for _, t, _ in saved])
        return _err('ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 500)
    return jsonify({'success': True})


def _fin_count():
    """طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھâ€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡/ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯ (ط¯ط± ط³ظ‚ظپ ظپط¶ط§غŒ ط¹ع©ط³ ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯)."""
    from models import FinanceFile
    return FinanceFile.query.count()

# D:\kargah\attachments.py  â€”  ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھ ظپط§ع©طھظˆط±/ط±ط³غŒط¯ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡ ظˆ ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯ (ط¹ع©ط³ غŒط§ PDF)طŒ ط¨ط§ ظ¾غŒط´â€Œظ†ظ…ط§غŒط´ ظˆ ط¯ط§ظ†ظ„ظˆط¯
import io
import os
import uuid

import jdatetime
from flask import Blueprint, g, jsonify, request, send_file
from flask_login import current_user
from sqlalchemy import event
from sqlalchemy.orm import object_session

from archive import _path, _remove_files, _save_image
from extensions import db
from finance import _err
from models import ArchivePhoto, Expense, FinanceFile, Income

bp = Blueprint('attach', __name__)
KINDS = {'expense': Expense, 'income': Income}
MAX_FILES = 10                       # ط­ط¯ط§ع©ط«ط± ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ‡ط± ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡/ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯
MAX_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024          # ط­ط¯ط§ع©ط«ط± ط­ط¬ظ… ظ‡ط± ظپط§غŒظ„


@event.listens_for(FinanceFile, 'after_delete')
def _queue_delete(mapper, connection, target):
    """ظپط§غŒظ„â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط±ظˆغŒ ط¯غŒط³ع© ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط§ط² commit ظ¾ط§ع© ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆظ†ط¯ (ظ‡ظ…ط§ظ† ط³ط§ط²ظˆع©ط§ط± ط¢ط±ط´غŒظˆ)."""
    s = object_session(target)
    if s is not None:
        s.info.setdefault('trash', []).extend([x for x in (target.filename, target.thumb) if x])


def _fj(f):
    return {'id': f.id, 'name': f.orig_name or 'ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھ', 'kind': f.kind, 'size': f.size_bytes or 0}


def _quota_error(adding):
    limit = current_user.photo_limit
    if limit is None:
        return None
    used = ArchivePhoto.query.count() + FinanceFile.query.count()
    if used + adding > limit:
        return 'ط³ظ‚ظپ ظپط¶ط§غŒ ط¹ع©ط³ ظˆ ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھ ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط±ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ† (%d ظپط§غŒظ„) ظ¾ط± ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯. ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط­ط°ظپ ط³ظ‚ظپطŒ ظ¾ظ„ظ† ط­ط±ظپظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ط¨ع¯غŒط±غŒط¯.' % limit
    return None


@bp.app_context_processor
def _inject():
    def files_of(kind, rec_id):
        """ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھâ€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ‡ط± ط±ط¯غŒظپظگ ظ„غŒط³طھ (غŒع©â€Œط¨ط§ط± ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ع©ظ„ طµظپط­ظ‡ ط§ط² ط¯غŒطھط§ط¨غŒط³ ط®ظˆط§ظ†ط¯ظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯)."""
        cache = g.get('_files_cache')
        if cache is None:
            cache = {'expense': {}, 'income': {}}
            yid = (request.view_args or {}).get('year_id')
            if yid:
                for model, col, key in ((Expense, FinanceFile.expense_id, 'expense'), (Income, FinanceFile.income_id, 'income')):
                    q = FinanceFile.query.join(model, col == model.id).filter(model.farming_year_id == yid).order_by(FinanceFile.id)
                    for f in q:
                        cache[key].setdefault(f.expense_id if key == 'expense' else f.income_id, []).append(_fj(f))
            g._files_cache = cache
        return cache.get(kind, {}).get(rec_id, [])
    return {'files_of': files_of, 'copyright_year': jdatetime.date.today().year}


@bp.route('/api/attachments/<kind>/<int:rec_id>', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def attachments(kind, rec_id):
    model = KINDS.get(kind)
    if not model:
        return _err('ظ†ظˆط¹ ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط§ط³طھ.', 404)
    rec = db.session.get(model, rec_id)
    if not rec:
        return _err('ط«ط¨طھ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify(success=True, files=[_fj(f) for f in rec.files])

    ups = [u for u in request.files.getlist('files') if u and u.filename]
    if not ups:
        return _err('ظپط§غŒظ„غŒ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.')
    if len(rec.files) + len(ups) > MAX_FILES:
        return _err('ط­ط¯ط§ع©ط«ط± %d ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ‡ط± ط«ط¨طھ ظ…ط¬ط§ط² ط§ط³طھ.' % MAX_FILES)
    quota = _quota_error(len(ups))
    if quota:
        return _err(quota, 403)

    sub = 'f%d' % rec.farming_year_id          # ظ¾ظˆط´ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ط¬ط¯ط§ ط§ط² ط¹ع©ط³â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط¢ط±ط´غŒظˆ
    written, rows = [], []
    try:
        for u in ups:
            data = u.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
            if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
                raise ValueError('آ«%sآ» ط¨ط²ط±ع¯â€Œطھط± ط§ط² غ¸ ظ…ع¯ط§ط¨ط§غŒطھ ط§ط³طھ.' % u.filename[:40])
            name = os.path.basename(u.filename)[:150]
            if data[:5] == b'%PDF-':
                rel = '%s/%s.pdf' % (sub, uuid.uuid4().hex)
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(_path(rel)), exist_ok=True)
                with open(_path(rel), 'wb') as fh:
                    fh.write(data)
                written.append(rel)
                rows.append(FinanceFile(filename=rel, thumb=None, orig_name=name, kind='pdf', mime='application/pdf', size_bytes=len(data)))
            else:
                u.stream = io.BytesIO(data)
                rel, thumb, size = _save_image(u, sub)      # ط§ط¹طھط¨ط§ط±ط³ظ†ط¬غŒ ط¨ط§ PillowطŒ ع©ظˆع†ع©â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ظˆ ط¨ظ†ط¯ط§ظ†ع¯ط´طھغŒ
                written += [rel, thumb]
                rows.append(FinanceFile(filename=rel, thumb=thumb, orig_name=name, kind='image', mime='image/jpeg', size_bytes=size))
        for r in rows:
            setattr(r, 'expense_id' if kind == 'expense' else 'income_id', rec.id)
            db.session.add(r)
        db.session.commit()
    except ValueError as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        _remove_files(written)
        return _err(str(e))
    return jsonify(success=True, files=[_fj(f) for f in rec.files])


@bp.route('/api/attachments/file/<int:fid>', methods=['DELETE'])
def delete_file(fid):
    f = db.session.get(FinanceFile, fid)
    if not f:
        return _err('ظ¾غŒظˆط³طھ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    db.session.delete(f)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(success=True)


def _serve(fid, thumb):
    f = db.session.get(FinanceFile, fid)
    rel = (f.thumb if thumb else f.filename) if f else None
    if not rel:
        return _err('ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    inline_pdf = f.kind == 'pdf' and not thumb
    resp = send_file(_path(rel), mimetype='application/pdf' if inline_pdf else 'image/jpeg',
                     as_attachment=request.args.get('download') == '1',
                     download_name=(f.orig_name or 'attachment') + ('' if (f.orig_name or '').lower().endswith(('.pdf', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp')) else ('.pdf' if inline_pdf else '.jpg')))
    resp.headers['X-Content-Type-Options'] = 'nosniff'
    resp.cache_control.private = True
    resp.cache_control.max_age = 3600
    return resp


@bp.route('/media/file/<int:fid>')
def file_media(fid):
    return _serve(fid, False)


@bp.route('/media/file/<int:fid>/thumb')
def file_thumb(fid):
    return _serve(fid, True)

# D:\kargah\auth.py  â€”  ظˆط±ظˆط¯ / ط®ط±ظˆط¬ / ط±ط§ظ‡â€Œط§ظ†ط¯ط§ط²غŒ ط§ظˆظ„غŒظ‡
import re
import time
from collections import defaultdict

from flask import Blueprint, render_template, request, redirect, url_for, jsonify
from flask_login import login_user, logout_user, current_user
from sqlalchemy import func

from extensions import db, login_manager
from models import User

bp = Blueprint('auth', __name__)

# endpointظ‡ط§غŒغŒ ع©ظ‡ ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ظˆط±ظˆط¯ ط¯ط± ط¯ط³طھط±ط³â€Œط§ظ†ط¯ (patchظ‡ط§غŒ ط¨ط¹ط¯غŒ ظ…ط«ظ„ manifest ط§غŒظ†ط¬ط§ ط§ط¶ط§ظپظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œع©ظ†ظ†ط¯)
OPEN_ENDPOINTS = {'auth.login', 'auth.setup', 'signup.register', 'static'}

EMAIL_RE = re.compile(r'^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$')
_fails = defaultdict(list)  # ip -> ط²ظ…ط§ظ† طھظ„ط§ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ†ط§ظ…ظˆظپظ‚ (ظ¾ط´طھ nginx ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ProxyFix ط§ط¶ط§ظپظ‡ ط´ظˆط¯)


@login_manager.user_loader
def load_user(user_id):
    return db.session.get(User, int(user_id))


@bp.before_app_request
def require_login():
    if request.endpoint is None or request.endpoint in OPEN_ENDPOINTS:
        return None
    if current_user.is_authenticated:
        return None
    if User.query.first() is None:
        return redirect(url_for('auth.setup'))
    if request.path.startswith('/api/'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'ط§ط¨طھط¯ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ط´ظˆغŒط¯'}), 401
    return redirect(url_for('auth.login', next=request.path))


def _safe_next(target):
    if target and target.startswith('/') and not target.startswith('//') and '\\' not in target:
        return target
    return url_for('index')


@bp.route('/setup', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def setup():
    """ظپظ‚ط· ظˆظ‚طھغŒ ظ‡غŒع† ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯: ط³ط§ط®طھ ط­ط³ط§ط¨ ظ…ط§ظ„ع© ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡."""
    if User.query.first() is not None:
        return redirect(url_for('auth.login'))
    error = None
    form = request.form
    if request.method == 'POST':
        name = (form.get('name') or '').strip()
        email = (form.get('email') or '').strip().lower() or None
        pw = form.get('password') or ''
        if not name:
            error = 'ظ†ط§ظ… ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.'
        elif email and not EMAIL_RE.match(email):
            error = 'ط§غŒظ…غŒظ„ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.'
        elif len(pw) < 6:
            error = 'ط±ظ…ط² ط¹ط¨ظˆط± ط¨ط§غŒط¯ ط­ط¯ط§ظ‚ظ„ غ¶ ع©ط§ط±ط§ع©طھط± ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.'
        elif pw != form.get('password2'):
            error = 'طھع©ط±ط§ط± ط±ظ…ط² ط¹ط¨ظˆط± غŒع©ط³ط§ظ† ظ†غŒط³طھ.'
        else:
            user = User(name=name, email=email)
            user.set_password(pw)
            db.session.add(user)
            db.session.commit()
            from tenancy import adopt_orphans
            adopt_orphans(user.id)   # ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ‚ط¯غŒظ…غŒ ط¨ظ‡ ظ…ط§ظ„ع© ظ…غŒâ€Œط±ط³ط¯
            login_user(user, remember=True)
            return redirect(url_for('index'))
    return render_template('setup.html', error=error, form=form)


@bp.route('/login', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def login():
    if User.query.first() is None:
        return redirect(url_for('auth.setup'))
    if current_user.is_authenticated:
        return redirect(url_for('index'))
    error = None
    if request.method == 'POST':
        ip = request.remote_addr or '-'
        now = time.time()
        _fails[ip] = [t for t in _fails[ip] if now - t < 600]
        if len(_fails[ip]) >= 5:
            error = 'طھظ„ط§ط´ ظ†ط§ظ…ظˆظپظ‚ ط²غŒط§ط¯ ط¨ظˆط¯ط› ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ ط¨ط¹ط¯ ط¯ظˆط¨ط§ط±ظ‡ ط§ظ…طھط­ط§ظ† ع©ظ†غŒط¯.'
        else:
            ident = (request.form.get('identifier') or '').strip()
            user = (User.query.filter(func.lower(User.email) == ident.lower()).first()
                    or User.query.filter_by(name=ident).first())
            if user and user.check_password(request.form.get('password') or ''):
                _fails.pop(ip, None)
                login_user(user, remember=True)
                return redirect(_safe_next(request.args.get('next')))
            _fails[ip].append(now)
            error = 'ظ†ط§ظ…/ط§غŒظ…غŒظ„ غŒط§ ط±ظ…ط² ط¹ط¨ظˆط± ط¯ط±ط³طھ ظ†غŒط³طھ.'
    return render_template('login.html', error=error)


@bp.route('/logout')
def logout():
    logout_user()
    return redirect(url_for('auth.login'))

# D:\kargah\dashboard.py  â€”  ط¯ط§ط´ط¨ظˆط±ط¯ + ظ†ط§ظˆط¨ط±غŒ ظ…ط´طھط±ع© طµظپط­ط§طھ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯
import jdatetime
from flask import Blueprint, render_template, request, current_app, url_for, abort

from extensions import db
from models import Workshop, FarmingYear
from services import year_totals, recent_items

bp = Blueprint('dashboard', __name__)

# (endpoint, ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ع©ظˆطھط§ظ‡, طھظˆط¶غŒط­, ط¢غŒع©ظˆظ†, ط±ظ†ع¯)
NAV = [
    ('dashboard.dashboard', 'ط¯ط§ط´ط¨ظˆط±ط¯', 'ظ†ظ…ط§غŒ ع©ظ„غŒ ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ', 'fa-th-large', 'green'),
    ('workshop_dashboard', 'ع©ط§ط± ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡', 'ط«ط¨طھ ع©ط§ط±طŒ ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط± ظˆ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯ ظ…طµط±ظپغŒ', 'fa-seedling', 'green'),
    ('finance.income_page', 'ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯', 'ط«ط¨طھ ظپط±ظˆط´ ط§ظ†ع¯ظˆط±طŒ ع©ط´ظ…ط´طŒ ط¢ط¨ط؛ظˆط±ظ‡ ظˆ...', 'fa-coins', 'orange'),
    ('finance.expense_page', 'ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡', 'ط¢ط¨غŒط§ط±غŒطŒ ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط±طŒ ع¯ط±ط¯ ظˆ ط±ظˆط؛ظ† ظˆ...', 'fa-receipt', 'red'),
    ('reports.report_page', 'ع¯ط²ط§ط±ط´ ظ…ط§ظ„غŒ', 'ط³ظˆط¯ ظˆ ط²غŒط§ظ† ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط²ظ‡ ط¯ظ„ط®ظˆط§ظ‡', 'fa-chart-pie', 'blue'),
    ('archive.archive_page', 'ط¢ط±ط´غŒظˆ', 'ط¹ع©ط³ ظˆ غŒط§ط¯ط¯ط§ط´طھ ظ…ط±ط§ط­ظ„ ط±ط´ط¯', 'fa-images', 'purple'),
    ('inventory.inventory_page', 'ط§ظ†ط¨ط§ط±', 'ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯غŒ ظˆ ظپط§ع©طھظˆط± ط®ط±غŒط¯ ط³ظ… ظˆ ع©ظˆط¯', 'fa-warehouse', 'blue'),
    ('finance.settings_page', 'طھظ†ط¸غŒظ…ط§طھ ظ…ط§ظ„غŒ', 'ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯ ظˆ ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡طŒ ظ‚غŒظ…طھ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯', 'fa-sliders-h', 'gray'),
    ('manage.manage_page', 'ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡', 'ط³ط§ظ„â€Œظ‡ط§طŒ ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھâ€Œظ‡ط§طŒ ع©ظˆط¯ظ‡ط§ ظˆ ط³ظ…ظˆظ…', 'fa-cog', 'gray'),
]


def build_nav(workshop_id, year_id):
    """طµظپط­ط§طھغŒ ع©ظ‡ ظ‡ظ†ظˆط² ظ†طµط¨ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§ظ†ط¯ href=None ظ…غŒâ€Œع¯غŒط±ظ†ط¯ ظˆ ط¯ط± ط¯ط§ط´ط¨ظˆط±ط¯ آ«ط¨ظ‡â€Œط²ظˆط¯غŒآ» ط¯غŒط¯ظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆظ†ط¯."""
    out = []
    for ep, short, desc, icon, tone in NAV:
        href = (url_for(ep, workshop_id=workshop_id, year_id=year_id)
                if ep in current_app.view_functions else None)
        out.append({'endpoint': ep, 'short': short, 'desc': desc, 'icon': icon,
                    'tone': tone, 'href': href})
    return out


@bp.app_context_processor
def inject_nav():
    va = request.view_args or {}
    if 'workshop_id' in va and 'year_id' in va:
        return {'nav_items': build_nav(va['workshop_id'], va['year_id'])}
    return {'nav_items': []}


@bp.app_template_filter('toman')
def toman(value):
    return '{:,}'.format(int(value or 0))


@bp.route('/dashboard/<int:workshop_id>/<int:year_id>')
def dashboard(workshop_id, year_id):
    workshop = db.get_or_404(Workshop, workshop_id)
    year = db.get_or_404(FarmingYear, year_id)
    if year.workshop_id != workshop.id:
        abort(404)
    all_years = FarmingYear.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop.id).order_by(FarmingYear.id).all()
    totals = year_totals(year.id)
    max_exp = max([i['amount'] for i in totals['expense_items']] or [1])
    return render_template(
        'dashboard.html', workshop=workshop, farming_year=year, all_years=all_years,
        today=jdatetime.date.today().strftime('%Y/%m/%d'),
        totals=totals, max_exp=max_exp, recent=recent_items(year.id))

# D:\kargah\finance.py  â€”  ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯طŒ ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡طŒ ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ظˆ ظ‚غŒظ…طھ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯
import re

import jdatetime
from flask import Blueprint, render_template, request, jsonify, abort

from extensions import db
from models import (Workshop, FarmingYear, Material, IncomeType, Income,
                    ExpenseCategory, Expense)

bp = Blueprint('finance', __name__)

KINDS = {
    'income': {'Type': IncomeType, 'Rec': Income, 'fk': 'income_type_id'},
    'expense': {'Type': ExpenseCategory, 'Rec': Expense, 'fk': 'category_id'},
}

_DIGITS = str.maketrans('غ°غ±غ²غ³غ´غµغ¶غ·غ¸غ¹ظ ظ،ظ¢ظ£ظ¤ظ¥ظ¦ظ§ظ¨ظ©', '01234567890123456789')


# ---------------- ع©ظ…ع©غŒâ€Œظ‡ط§ ----------------
def _err(msg, code=400):
    return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': msg}), code


def _num(v, as_int=True):
    """ط¹ط¯ط¯ (ط¨ط§ ط§ط±ظ‚ط§ظ… ظپط§ط±ط³غŒ/ع©ط§ظ…ط§) -> int غŒط§ floatط› ط®ط§ظ„غŒ -> None."""
    if v is None or str(v).strip() == '':
        return None
    s = str(v).translate(_DIGITS).replace(',', '').replace('طŒ', '').replace('ظ«', '.').strip()
    try:
        f = float(s)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError('ط¹ط¯ط¯ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ط´ط¯ظ‡ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    if f < 0:
        raise ValueError('ط¹ط¯ط¯ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œطھظˆط§ظ†ط¯ ظ…ظ†ظپغŒ ط¨ط§ط´ط¯.')
    return int(round(f)) if as_int else f


def _date(v):
    s = str(v or '').translate(_DIGITS).strip()
    m = re.match(r'^(\d{4})[/\-.](\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{1,2})$', s)
    if not m:
        raise ValueError('طھط§ط±غŒط® ط±ط§ ظ…ط«ظ„ 1405/03/21 ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    y, mo, d = map(int, m.groups())
    try:
        jdatetime.date(y, mo, d)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError('طھط§ط±غŒط® ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    return '%04d/%02d/%02d' % (y, mo, d)


def _page_ctx(workshop_id, year_id):
    workshop = db.get_or_404(Workshop, workshop_id)
    year = db.get_or_404(FarmingYear, year_id)
    if year.workshop_id != workshop.id:
        abort(404)
    all_years = FarmingYear.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop.id).order_by(FarmingYear.id).all()
    return {'workshop': workshop, 'farming_year': year, 'all_years': all_years,
            'today': jdatetime.date.today().strftime('%Y/%m/%d')}


def _item(kind, r):
    t = r.income_type if kind == 'income' else r.category
    d = {'id': r.id, 'date': r.date_shamsi, 'type_id': t.id, 'type_name': t.name,
         'amount': r.amount or 0, 'description': r.description or ''}
    if kind == 'income':
        d.update(unit=t.unit or '', quantity=r.quantity, unit_price=r.unit_price, buyer=r.buyer or '', is_gift=bool(r.is_gift))
    return d


def _type_json(t):
    return {'id': t.id, 'name': t.name, 'unit': getattr(t, 'unit', '') or ''}


# ---------------- طµظپط­ط§طھ ----------------
def _ledger(kind, workshop_id, year_id):
    ctx = _page_ctx(workshop_id, year_id)
    cfg = KINDS[kind]
    Rec, Type, fk = cfg['Rec'], cfg['Type'], cfg['fk']
    all_types = Type.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop_id).order_by(Type.id).all()
    filter_type = request.args.get('type', type=int)
    q = Rec.query.filter_by(farming_year_id=year_id)
    if filter_type:
        q = q.filter(getattr(Rec, fk) == filter_type)
    items = [_item(kind, r) for r in q.order_by(Rec.date_shamsi.desc(), Rec.id.desc()).all()]
    return render_template(
        'finance_ledger.html', kind=kind, is_income=(kind == 'income'), items=items,
        total=sum(i['amount'] for i in items if not i.get('is_gift')),
        gift_total=sum(i['amount'] for i in items if i.get('is_gift')),
        gift_count=sum(1 for i in items if i.get('is_gift')), filter_type=filter_type,
        all_types=[_type_json(t) for t in all_types],
        types=[_type_json(t) for t in all_types if t.is_active], **ctx)


@bp.route('/finance/<int:workshop_id>/<int:year_id>/income')
def income_page(workshop_id, year_id):
    return _ledger('income', workshop_id, year_id)


@bp.route('/finance/<int:workshop_id>/<int:year_id>/expense')
def expense_page(workshop_id, year_id):
    return _ledger('expense', workshop_id, year_id)


@bp.route('/finance/<int:workshop_id>/<int:year_id>/settings')
def settings_page(workshop_id, year_id):
    ctx = _page_ctx(workshop_id, year_id)
    return render_template(
        'finance_settings.html',
        income_types=IncomeType.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop_id).order_by(IncomeType.id).all(),
        expense_cats=ExpenseCategory.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop_id).order_by(ExpenseCategory.id).all(),
        materials=Material.query.filter_by(workshop_id=workshop_id).order_by(Material.name).all(), **ctx)


# ---------------- API: ط«ط¨طھâ€Œظ‡ط§ ----------------
def _apply(kind, rec, data, year):
    cfg = KINDS[kind]
    rec.date_shamsi = _date(data.get('date'))
    t = db.session.get(cfg['Type'], _num(data.get('type_id')) or 0)
    if not t or t.workshop_id != year.workshop_id:
        raise ValueError('ظ†ظˆط¹ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨â€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ظ†غŒط³طھ.')
    setattr(rec, cfg['fk'], t.id)
    rec.description = (data.get('description') or '').strip()
    amount = _num(data.get('amount'))
    if kind == 'income':
        q, p = _num(data.get('quantity'), False), _num(data.get('unit_price'))
        if amount is None and q is not None and p is not None:
            amount = int(round(q * p))
        if amount and q and not p:
            p = int(round(amount / q))      # ظ‚غŒظ…طھ ظ‡ط± ظˆط§ط­ط¯ (ظ…ط«ظ„ط§ظ‹ ع©غŒظ„ظˆغŒغŒ) ط§ط² ظ…ط¨ظ„ط؛ ع©ظ„ ظˆ ظ…ظ‚ط¯ط§ط±
        rec.quantity, rec.unit_price = q, p
        rec.buyer = (data.get('buyer') or '').strip()[:100]
        g = data.get('is_gift')
        rec.is_gift = (g.strip().lower() in ('1', 'true', 'on', 'yes')) if isinstance(g, str) else bool(g)
    if not amount:
        raise ValueError('ظ…ط¨ظ„ط؛ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    rec.amount = amount


@bp.route('/api/finance/<kind>', methods=['POST'])
def api_create(kind):
    if kind not in KINDS:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    try:
        year = db.session.get(FarmingYear, _num(data.get('year_id')) or 0)
        if not year:
            return _err('ط³ط§ظ„ ط²ط±ط§ط¹غŒ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
        rec = KINDS[kind]['Rec'](farming_year_id=year.id)
        _apply(kind, rec, data, year)
    except ValueError as e:
        return _err(str(e))
    db.session.add(rec)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': rec.id})


@bp.route('/api/finance/<kind>/<int:rec_id>', methods=['PUT', 'DELETE'])
def api_update(kind, rec_id):
    if kind not in KINDS:
        abort(404)
    rec = db.session.get(KINDS[kind]['Rec'], rec_id)
    if not rec:
        return _err('ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    if request.method == 'DELETE':
        db.session.delete(rec)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    try:
        _apply(kind, rec, request.get_json(silent=True) or {}, db.session.get(FarmingYear, rec.farming_year_id))
    except ValueError as e:
        db.session.rollback()
        return _err(str(e))
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


# ---------------- API: ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¹ ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯ / ط¯ط³طھظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡ ----------------
@bp.route('/api/finance/types/<kind>', methods=['POST'])
def api_type_create(kind):
    if kind not in KINDS:
        abort(404)
    Type = KINDS[kind]['Type']
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    try:
        wid = _num(data.get('workshop_id')) or 0
    except ValueError as e:
        return _err(str(e))
    name = (data.get('name') or '').strip()[:100]
    if not db.session.get(Workshop, wid):
        return _err('ع©ط§ط±ع¯ط§ظ‡ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    if not name:
        return _err('ظ†ط§ظ… ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    if Type.query.filter_by(workshop_id=wid, name=name).first():
        return _err('ط§غŒظ† ظ†ط§ظ… ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.')
    t = Type(name=name, workshop_id=wid)
    if kind == 'income':
        t.unit = (data.get('unit') or 'ع©غŒظ„ظˆع¯ط±ظ…').strip()[:20]
    db.session.add(t)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': t.id})


@bp.route('/api/finance/types/<kind>/<int:type_id>', methods=['PUT', 'DELETE'])
def api_type_update(kind, type_id):
    if kind not in KINDS:
        abort(404)
    cfg = KINDS[kind]
    t = db.session.get(cfg['Type'], type_id)
    if not t:
        return _err('ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    if request.method == 'DELETE':
        used = cfg['Rec'].query.filter(getattr(cfg['Rec'], cfg['fk']) == t.id).count()
        if used:
            return _err('ط§غŒظ† ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ط¯ط± %d ط«ط¨طھ ط§ط³طھظپط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯ظ‡ط› ط¨ظ‡â€Œط¬ط§غŒ ط­ط°ظپطŒ ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„ط´ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.' % used)
        db.session.delete(t)
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    name = (data.get('name') or '').strip()[:100]
    if not name:
        return _err('ظ†ط§ظ… ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.')
    clash = cfg['Type'].query.filter_by(workshop_id=t.workshop_id, name=name).first()
    if clash and clash.id != t.id:
        return _err('ط§غŒظ† ظ†ط§ظ… ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ظ‹ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯.')
    t.name = name
    t.is_active = bool(data.get('is_active', True))
    if kind == 'income':
        t.unit = (data.get('unit') or t.unit or 'ع©غŒظ„ظˆع¯ط±ظ…').strip()[:20]
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


# ---------------- API: ظ‚غŒظ…طھ ظ…ظˆط§ط¯ ----------------
@bp.route('/api/finance/material-price/<int:material_id>', methods=['PUT'])
def api_material_price(material_id):
    m = db.session.get(Material, material_id)
    if not m:
        return _err('ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ظ¾غŒط¯ط§ ظ†ط´ط¯.', 404)
    try:
        m.default_price = _num((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('price')) or 0
    except ValueError as e:
        return _err(str(e))
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})
