{% block scripts %}
<script>
    const WORKSHOP_ID = {{ workshop.id }};
    const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
    document.querySelectorAll('.m-price').forEach(ui.money.bind(ui));

    function showTab(name) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.toggle('on', t.dataset.tab === name));
        document.querySelectorAll('.pane').forEach(p => { p.hidden = p.id !== 'pane-' + name; });
        history.replaceState(null, '', '#' + name);
    }
    showTab(['years', 'acts', 'mats'].includes(location.hash.slice(1)) ? location.hash.slice(1) : 'years');
    const done = (res, okMsg, reload) => {
        if (!res.success) return ui.toast(res.error || 'ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ظ†ط´ط¯.', true);
        if (reload) location.reload(); else ui.toast(okMsg);
    };

    // ---- ط³ط§ظ„â€Œظ‡ط§ ----
    async function addYear() {
        done(await ui.call('POST', '/api/add_farming_year', {workshop_id: WORKSHOP_ID, year: $('newYear').value}), '', true);
    }
    async function delYear(id) {
        if (!await ui.confirm('ظ‡ظ…ظ‡â€ŒغŒ ع©ط§ط±ظ‡ط§طŒ ط¯ط±ط¢ظ…ط¯ظ‡ط§طŒ ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§ ظˆ ط¹ع©ط³â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط§غŒظ† ط³ط§ظ„ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ‡ظ…غŒط´ظ‡ ط­ط°ظپ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯. ط§ط¯ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظ…غŒâ€Œط¯ظ‡غŒط¯طں', {danger: true, ok: 'ط­ط°ظپ ط³ط§ظ„'})) return;
        done(await ui.call('DELETE', `/api/delete_year/${id}?confirm=1`), '', true);
    }

    // ---- ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھâ€Œظ‡ط§ ----
    async function addAct() {
        done(await ui.call('POST', '/api/add_activity', {workshop_id: WORKSHOP_ID, name: $('newAct').value, has_material: $('newActMat').checked}), '', true);
    }
    async function saveAct(btn) {
        const row = btn.closest('.type-row');
        done(await ui.call('PUT', `/api/update_activity/${row.dataset.id}`, {name: row.querySelector('.t-name').value, has_material: row.querySelector('.a-mat').checked}), 'ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯');
    }
    async function delAct(btn) {
        if (!await ui.confirm('ط§غŒظ† ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ ط­ط°ظپ ط´ظˆط¯طں', {danger: true})) return;
        const res = await ui.call('DELETE', `/api/delete_activity/${btn.closest('.type-row').dataset.id}`);
        if (res.success && res.message) ui.toast(res.message);
        if (res.success) setTimeout(() => location.reload(), res.message ? 1400 : 0); else ui.toast(res.error || 'ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ط´ط¯.', true);
    }

    // ---- ع©ظˆط¯ظ‡ط§ ظˆ ط³ظ…ظˆظ… ----
    async function addMat() {
        done(await ui.call('POST', '/api/add_material', {
            workshop_id: WORKSHOP_ID, name: $('newMatName').value, type: $('newMatType').value,
            unit: $('newMatUnit').value, default_price: ui.norm($('newMatPrice').value)
        }), '', true);
    }
    async function saveMat(btn) {
        const row = btn.closest('.type-row');
        done(await ui.call('PUT', `/api/update_material/${row.dataset.id}`, {
            name: row.querySelector('.t-name').value, type: row.querySelector('.m-type').value,
            unit: row.querySelector('.m-unit').value, default_price: ui.norm(row.querySelector('.m-price').value)
        }), 'ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ط´ط¯');
    }
    async function delMat(btn) {
        if (!await ui.confirm('ط§غŒظ† ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط­ط°ظپ ط´ظˆط¯طں', {danger: true})) return;
        done(await ui.call('DELETE', `/api/delete_material/${btn.closest('.type-row').dataset.id}`), '', true);
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% macro work_cost(w) -%}
{%- set ns = namespace(t=(w.total_wage or 0) + (w.food_cost or 0)) -%}
{%- for u in w.materials_used if u.material -%}
{%- set ns.t = ns.t + ((u.quantity or 0) * (u.unit_price if u.unit_price is not none else (u.material.default_price or 0)))|round|int -%}
{%- endfor -%}
{{ ns.t }}
{%- endmacro %}

    <div class="field">

        <div class="stepper">

            <input id="wCount" inputmode="numeric" value="0" autocomplete="off">
            <button type="button" onclick="step(1)" aria-label="ط¨غŒط´طھط±">+</button>

    <div class="tabs mini" id="wMode" role="tablist">

        <input id="wFood" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off"></label>

    <div class="sub-card" id="matBox" hidden>
        <h4><i class="fas fa-flask"></i> ظ…ظˆط§ط¯ ظ…طµط±ظپغŒ</h4>
        <div class="row-inline">
            <select id="mSel" aria-label="ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡">
                <option value="">ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط±ط§ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ع©ظ†غŒط¯â€¦</option>
                {% for m in materials %}<option value="{{ m.id }}" data-name="{{ m.name }}" data-unit="{{ m.unit }}" data-stock="{{ '%g'|format(stock_of(m.id)) }}">{{ m.name }} ({{ m.unit }}) â€” ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯غŒ {{ '%g'|format(stock_of(m.id)) }}</option>{% endfor %}

        </div>
        <ul class="picked" id="pickedList"></ul>

        <details id="nmBox" style="margin-top: 10px">
            <summary class="muted" style="cursor: pointer">ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ع©ظ‡ ط¯ط± ظ„غŒط³طھ ظ†غŒط³طھطں</summary>
            <div class="row-inline" style="flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 8px">
                <input id="nmName" placeholder="ظ†ط§ظ… ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡" aria-label="ظ†ط§ظ… ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡">
                <select id="nmType" aria-label="ظ†ظˆط¹"><option>ع©ظˆط¯</option><option>ط³ظ…</option><option>ع©ظˆط¯ ظ…ط§غŒط¹</option></select>
                <select id="nmUnit" aria-label="ظˆط§ط­ط¯"><option>ع©غŒظ„ظˆع¯ط±ظ…</option><option>ظ„غŒطھط±</option><option>ط¹ط¯ط¯</option></select>
                <button type="button" class="btn-sm" onclick="newMat()">ط³ط§ط®طھ</button>
            </div>
        </details>
    </div>

    <label class="field"><span>طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ (ط§ط®طھغŒط§ط±غŒ)</span><textarea id="wDesc" rows="2"></textarea></label>
    <div id="wErr" class="alert" hidden></div>
    <div class="btn-row">
        <button type="button" class="btn secondary" onclick="workDlg.close()">ط§ظ†طµط±ط§ظپ</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn" id="wSave" onclick="saveWork()">ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡</button>
    </div>

{% block scripts %}
<script>
    const YEAR_ID = {{ farming_year.id }}, WORKSHOP_ID = {{ workshop.id }};
    const TODAY = '{{ today }}', YESTERDAY = '{{ yesterday }}';
    const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
    const dlg = $('workDlg');
    let editId = null, picked = [], hadMats = false, keepTotal = null, origUse = {}, wMode = 'per';

    ui.money($('wWage')); ui.money($('wFood')); ui.backdropClose(dlg);

    const actInput = () => document.querySelector('input[name=act]:checked');
    const needsMat = () => { const a = actInput(); return !!a && a.dataset.mat === '1'; };
    const num = id => parseInt(ui.norm($(id).value), 10) || 0;
    function total() {
        if (keepTotal !== null) return keepTotal;
        const c = num('wCount'), v = num('wWage');
        return wMode === 'total' ? v : (c > 0 ? c * v : v);   // آ«ظ…ط¨ظ„ط؛ ع©ظ„آ» ط±ط§ ظ…ط³طھظ‚غŒظ… ط¨ط¯ظ‡غŒط¯ غŒط§ آ«ظ†ظپط±غŒآ» طھط§ ط¶ط±ط¨ ط´ظˆط¯
    }
    function setMode(m) {
        if (m === wMode) return;
        const T = total(), c = num('wCount');
        wMode = m; keepTotal = null;
        $('wWage').value = !T ? '' : ui.fmt(String(m === 'total' ? T : (c > 0 ? Math.round(T / c) : T)));
        $('wWageLbl').textContent = m === 'total' ? 'ظ…ط¨ظ„ط؛ ع©ظ„ ط¯ط³طھظ…ط²ط¯ (طھظˆظ…ط§ظ†)' : 'ط¯ط³طھظ…ط²ط¯ ظ‡ط± ظ†ظپط± (طھظˆظ…ط§ظ†)';
        document.querySelectorAll('#wMode .tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.m === m));
        refresh();
    }
    function refresh() {
        $('matBox').hidden = !(needsMat() || picked.length > 0);
        const T = total(), cnt = num('wCount');
        $('wTotalLbl').textContent = wMode === 'total' ? 'ط¯ط³طھظ…ط²ط¯ ظ‡ط± ظ†ظپط±' : 'ط¬ظ…ط¹ ط¯ط³طھظ…ط²ط¯';
        $('wTotal').textContent = wMode === 'total' ? (cnt > 0 ? Math.round(T / cnt).toLocaleString('en-US') : 'â€”') : T.toLocaleString('en-US');
        stockWarn();
    }
    function touch() { keepTotal = null; refresh(); }
    function step(d) { $('wCount').value = Math.max(0, num('wCount') + d); touch(); }
    function showErr(msg) { $('wErr').textContent = msg || ''; $('wErr').hidden = !msg; }
    // ظ‡ط´ط¯ط§ط± ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ط§ظ†ط¨ط§ط± (ظ…ط§ظ†ط¹ ط«ط¨طھ ظ†ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯ط› ظپظ‚ط· ط®ط¨ط± ظ…غŒâ€Œط¯ظ‡ط¯)
    function stockWarn() {
        const box = $('stockWarn'); if (!box) return;
        const need = {};
        picked.forEach(m => { need[m.material_id] = (need[m.material_id] || 0) + m.quantity; });
        box.innerHTML = '';
        Object.keys(need).forEach(id => {
            const o = [...$('mSel').options].find(x => x.value === String(id)); if (!o) return;
            const avail = parseFloat(o.dataset.stock || '0') + (origUse[String(id)] || 0);
            if (need[id] > avail + 1e-9) {
                const d = document.createElement('div');
                d.textContent = 'âڑ  ' + o.dataset.name + ': ط¯ط± ط§ظ†ط¨ط§ط± ' + (+avail.toFixed(2)) + ' ' + o.dataset.unit + ' ظ‡ط³طھطŒ ' + (+need[id].toFixed(2)) + ' ظ…غŒâ€Œط®ظˆط§ظ‡غŒط¯ (ع©ط³ط±غŒ ' + (+(need[id] - avail).toFixed(2)) + ')';
                box.appendChild(d);
            }
        });
        box.hidden = !box.children.length;
    }
    $('wCount').addEventListener('input', touch);
    $('wWage').addEventListener('input', touch);
    $('actGrid').addEventListener('change', refresh);

    function renderPicked() {
        const ul = $('pickedList'); ul.innerHTML = '';
        picked.forEach((m, i) => {
            const li = document.createElement('li');
            const s = document.createElement('span'); s.textContent = `${m.name}: ${m.quantity} ${m.unit}`;
            const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'icon-sm danger';
            b.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-times"></i>';
            b.onclick = () => { picked.splice(i, 1); renderPicked(); };
            li.append(s, b); ul.appendChild(li);
        });
        refresh();
    }
    function addMat() {
        const o = $('mSel').selectedOptions[0], q = parseFloat(ui.norm($('mQty').value));
        if (!o || !o.value) return showErr('ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط±ط§ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.');
        if (!(q > 0)) return showErr('ظ…ظ‚ط¯ط§ط± ظ…طµط±ظپ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.');
        showErr('');
        const ex = picked.find(m => String(m.material_id) === o.value);
        if (ex) ex.quantity = +(ex.quantity + q).toFixed(3);
        else picked.push({material_id: +o.value, name: o.dataset.name, unit: o.dataset.unit, quantity: q});
        $('mSel').value = ''; $('mQty').value = '';
        renderPicked();
    }
    async function newMat() {
        const name = $('nmName').value.trim(), unit = $('nmUnit').value;
        if (!name) return showErr('ظ†ط§ظ… ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط±ط§ ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.');
        const res = await ui.call('POST', '/api/add_material', {workshop_id: WORKSHOP_ID, name, type: $('nmType').value, unit});
        if (!res.success) return showErr(res.error || 'ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط¶ط§ظپظ‡ ظ†ط´ط¯.');
        const o = new Option(`${name} (${unit}) â€” ظ…ظˆط¬ظˆط¯غŒ 0`, res.id); o.dataset.name = name; o.dataset.unit = unit; o.dataset.stock = '0';
        $('mSel').add(o); $('mSel').value = String(res.id);
        $('nmName').value = ''; $('nmBox').open = false; $('mQty').focus(); showErr('');
    }

    async function openWork(item) {
        editId = item ? item.id : null; picked = []; hadMats = false; keepTotal = null; origUse = {};
        wMode = 'per'; document.querySelectorAll('#wMode .tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.m === 'per'));
        $('wWageLbl').textContent = 'ط¯ط³طھظ…ط²ط¯ ظ‡ط± ظ†ظپط± (طھظˆظ…ط§ظ†)';
        $('wTitle').textContent = item ? 'ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ع©ط§ط±' : 'ط«ط¨طھ ع©ط§ط± ط¬ط¯غŒط¯';
        showErr('');
        document.querySelectorAll('.pick.tmp').forEach(e => e.remove());
        document.querySelectorAll('input[name=act]').forEach(r => { r.checked = false; });
        if (item && item.activity_id && !document.querySelector(`input[name=act][value="${item.activity_id}"]`)) {
            const l = document.createElement('label'); l.className = 'pick tmp';
            l.innerHTML = '<input type="radio" name="act"><span></span>';
            l.querySelector('input').value = item.activity_id;
            l.querySelector('span').textContent = (item.act_name || 'ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ') + ' (ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„)';
            $('actGrid').appendChild(l);
        }
        if (item) { const r = document.querySelector(`input[name=act][value="${item.activity_id}"]`); if (r) r.checked = true; }
        const c = item ? item.workers : 0;
        $('wDate').value = item ? item.date : TODAY;
        $('wCount').value = c;
        $('wWage').value = item ? ui.fmt(String(c > 0 ? Math.round(item.wage / c) : item.wage)) : '';
        keepTotal = item ? item.wage : null;
        $('wFood').value = item && item.food ? ui.fmt(String(item.food)) : '';
        $('wDesc').value = item ? item.desc : '';
        renderPicked();
        dlg.showModal();
        if (item) {
            const r = await ui.call('GET', '/api/get_work_materials/' + item.id);
            if (Array.isArray(r)) {
                picked = r.map(m => ({material_id: m.material_id, name: m.name, unit: m.unit, quantity: m.quantity}));
                r.forEach(m => { origUse[String(m.material_id)] = m.quantity; });
                hadMats = picked.length > 0; renderPicked();
            }
        }
    }

    async function saveWork() {
        const a = actInput();
        if (!a) return showErr('ظپط¹ط§ظ„غŒطھ ط±ط§ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ع©ظ†غŒط¯.');
        const mats = picked.map(m => ({material_id: m.material_id, quantity: m.quantity}));
        if (needsMat() && !mats.length &&
            !await ui.confirm('ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ. ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ظ…ظˆط§ط¯ ظ…طµط±ظپغŒ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ظˆط¯طں', {ok: 'ط¨ظ„ظ‡طŒ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ظˆط¯'})) return;
        const body = {
            date: $('wDate').value, activity_id: a.value, worker_count: num('wCount'), total_wage: total(),
            food_cost: num('wFood'), description: $('wDesc').value, farming_year_id: YEAR_ID
        };
        const wantsMat = needsMat() || mats.length > 0;
        const btn = $('wSave'); btn.disabled = true; showErr('');
        let res;
        if (editId) {
            res = await ui.call('POST', '/api/update_work', Object.assign({work_id: editId}, body));
            if (res.success && (wantsMat || hadMats)) res = await ui.call('POST', '/api/save_material_usage', {work_id: editId, materials: mats});
        } else if (wantsMat) {
            res = await ui.call('POST', '/api/save_work_temp', body);
            if (res.success) {
                const id = res.work_id;
                res = await ui.call('POST', '/api/save_material_usage', {work_id: id, materials: mats});
                if (!res.success) await ui.call('DELETE', '/api/delete_temp_work/' + id);
            }
        } else {
            res = await ui.call('POST', '/api/save_work_final', body);
        }
        btn.disabled = false;
        if (res.success) location.reload(); else showErr(res.error || 'ط°ط®غŒط±ظ‡ ظ†ط´ط¯.');
    }

    async function delWork(id) {
        if (!await ui.confirm('ط§غŒظ† ع©ط§ط± ط­ط°ظپ ط´ظˆط¯طں', {danger: true})) return;
        const r = await ui.call('DELETE', '/api/delete_work/' + id);
        if (r.success) location.reload(); else ui.toast(r.error || 'ط­ط°ظپ ظ†ط´ط¯.', true);
    }

    function filterWork(btn, id) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.chips-scroll .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', c === btn));
        document.querySelectorAll('#workList .ledger-item').forEach(li => { li.hidden = !!id && li.dataset.act !== String(id); });
    }
</script>
